Date: 23-02-2024.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Barakanalu village of
Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called Barakanalu Lambani Tanda as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Barakanalu village for conversion of Barakanalu Lambani Tanda unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/ extension of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

(7545)

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Barakanalu Lambani Tanda
village of Chikkanayakanahalli taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form
hamlet/part within the boundary of Barakanalu Revenue village and same will here
in after be named as called Barakanalu Lambani Tanda of Barakanalu village
Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk Tumkur District

with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area( Gramatana) of Barakanalu

Lambani Tanda hamlet/part of Barakanalu village in Chikkanayakana halli Taluk

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 148 PFH 2022 Dated: 06-12-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Anivala village of Honnavalli hobli Tiptur
Taluk in Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as
Sampigepura sub village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
No.RD 148 PFH 2022 dated 06-12-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all
the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkuru
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 06-12-2022.
And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Sampigepura village of Tiptur Taluk of
Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/part with in the boundary of
Anivala revenue village and same will here in after be named as called Sampigepura
of Anivala village Tiptur Taluk of Tumkuru District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is converted as habitation area( Gramatana) of Sampigepura hamlet/part of
Anivala village in Tiptur taluk of Tumkur District.
The proposal specified above issued under section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964) shall come into effect from the
date of final notification.

By order in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No No RD 22 PFH 2021, Dated : 03 -02-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Kumbarkoppa village of Alnavar Taluk and
Dharwad District and constituting a new revenue village called Siddapur was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 157 Part IV-A dated : 03-
02-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Dharwad district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 07-02-2022And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Kumbarkoppa village of Alnavar Taluk and Dharwad District to form a new revenue
village called as Siddapur of AlnavarTaluk and Dharwad District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of SATHURU Village of
Harapanahalli Taluk of Vijayanagara District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called CHOUDAPURA as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
SATHURU village for conversion of CHOUDAPURA unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said

Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be
affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection
or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
Vijayanagara District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called CHOUDAPURA village of
Harapanahalli Taluk of Vijayanagara District convert the same to form hamlet
within the boundary of SATHURU Revenue village and same will here in after be
named as called CHOUDAPURA of SATHURU village in Harapanahalli Taluk of
Vijayanagara District.

in Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as
Marasarakottige sub village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
No.RD 552 KAMGRARA 2017 dated 27-07-2017 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 28-07-
2017.

And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

table shall be declared as habitation area of Marasarakottige village of Turuvekere
Taluk of Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet with in the boundary of

PR-550

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS/29/2023-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1279284 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 21 -02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Dharmapura Village of
Hiriyuru taluk Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Aralikere as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Dharmapura of Hiriyuru taluk in Chitradurga District to form a new
revenue village called Aralikere of Hiriyuru taluk Chitradurga District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of ALABURU village of
KOTTURU Taluk VIJAYANAGARA District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called HONNIHALLI AND DIBBADAHALLI as specified in schedule
along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or

suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
VIJAYANAGARA District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village ALABURU of Kotturu Taluku in VIJAYANAGARA District to form a new
revenue village called DIBBADAHALLI of Kotturu Taluku and VIJAYANAGARA
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of ByluruVillage of Hannur
Taluk, Chamarajnagar district District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Andekurubana doddi as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chamarajnagar
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Byluru Village of Hannur Taluk,Chamarajnagar district to form a New
revenue village called as Andekurubana doddi of Hannur Taluk and Chamarajnagar
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/306/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1338948 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 28-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Navali Village of Hadagali
Taluk of Vijayanagara District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Halehonnuru as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Navali village for conversion of Halehonnuru unrecorded habitation in to hamlet of
the said revenue village.
Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be
affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection
or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
Vijayanagara District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Halehonnuru village of
Hadagali Taluk of Vijayanagara District convert the same to form hamlet/part
within the boundary of Navali Revenue village and same will here in after be named
as called Halehonnuru of Navali village in Hadagali Taluk of Vijayanagara
District.

with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area (Gramatana) of Halehonnuru
hamlet of Navali village in Hadagali Taluk of Vijayanagara District

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as
the State Government may specify by Notification issued under section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 ( Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964 )

Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 28-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Mangala Village of Mandya
Taluk, Mandya District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Mangalathittu as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Mandya
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Mangala village of Mandya District Mandya Taluk to form a new revenue village
called Mangalathittu Village of Mandya Taluk, Mandya District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS0ADMN/274/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1332496 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001,
Date:28-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 95 PFH 2022 Dated:22-06-2022
regarding altering the limits of Madenahalli village of Maddur Taluk and Mandya
District and constituting a new revenue village called Tittamaranahalli was published
as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act
No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 95 PFH 2022 Dated: 22-06-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Mandya district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 22-07-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Madenahalli village of Maddur Taluk and Mandya District to form a new
revenue village called as Tittamaranahalli village of Maddur Taluk and Mandya
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Date: 17-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of DANAYAKANAKERE
village of HOSAPETE Taluk VIJAYANAGARA District and to constitute a new
revenue villages called DEVALAPURA as specified in schedule along with
maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
VIJAYANAGARA District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said

village of DANAYAKANAKERE of HOSAPETE Taluku and VIJAYANAGARA
District to form a new revenue village called DEVALAPURA of HOSAPETE Taluku
and VIJAYANAGARA District along with the survey numbers included in column (4)
thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS/9/2023-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1268378 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:22-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RD 323 PFH 2022 Dated:15-11-2022
regarding altering the limits of Cheelaganahalli village of Koratagere Taluk in
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as Yadagere sub
village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No.RD 323 PFH 2022
Dated 15-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 21-11-2022. And whereas, And whereas, no objections and suggestions have
been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Yadagere village of Koratagere Taluk of
Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/ part with in the boundary of
Cheelaganahalli revenue village and same will here in after be named as called
Yadagere of Cheelaganahalli village in Koratagere Taluk of Tumkuru District.

with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 552 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated: 22-07-2017 regarding altering the limits of Kodagihalli villages of
Turuvekere Taluk in Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village
called as Marasarakottige sub village was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No.RD 552 KAMGRARA 2017 dated 27-07-2017 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Tumkuru district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 28-07-2017.

And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Marasarakottige village of Turuvekere
Taluk of Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet with in the boundary of
Kodagihalli revenue village and same will here in after be named as called
Marasarakottige Of Kodagihalli villages Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

[
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 491 KAMGRARA 2017, Dated :
14.06.2017 regarding altering the limits of Vatalu village of T.Narasipura Taluk
and Mysuru District and constituting a new revenue village called Vatalupura was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No.152 Part IV-A dated :
20.07.2017 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Mysuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 02.08.2017 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the
areas specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of

the said village of T.Narasipura Taluk and Mysuru District to form a new revenue
village called as Vatalupura of T.Narasipura Taluk and Mysuru District. The new
survey numbers which will form part of this new village are as specified in column (4)
thereof.

Date:22-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 97 GLH 2022, Dated : 22-06-2022
regarding altering the limits of JANNURU village of Chamrajnagara Taluk and
Chamrajnagara District and constituting a new revenue village called JANNURU
HOSURU was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 9404 Part IV-A
dated : 11-07-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chamrajnagara district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:20.07.2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village JANNURU village of Chamrajnagara Taluk and Chamrajnagara District to
form a new revenue village called as JANNURU HOSURU of Chamrajnagara Taluk
and Chamrajnagara District. along with the survey numbers included in column
(4) thereof.
